Body, status, and button treatments are later\n            // slices; this slice is headings only.\n            Text(title.uppercased())\n                .font(.system(size: 15, weight: .bold))\n                .tracking(1.2)\n                .accessibilityLabel(title)\n        } else {\n            Text(title)\n                .font(.system(size: 15, weight: .bold))\n        }\n    }\n}\n",
